# Log

Log started at 2026-04-21T00:00:00.000000

## [2026-05-21] create | software-factory — Architecture concept page: stress-test of GSD-2 + Forgejo + Crumbs HITL pipeline, surfaced 5 open questions about execution model, memory, orchestrator, feedback loop, and canonical state ownership. Sources: GSD-2 docs, Beads, lobehub analysis.

## [2026-06-01] create | [[temporal-durable-execution]] — Synthesized research page: Temporal as execution plane. Corroborated Hal's notes (3 _workflow*.md files) with web research. Deployed Temporal Server (Docker auto-setup, :7233) + Web UI (:8233). Note: Helm chart ringpop membership is unreliable on single-node k3s — Docker auto-setup with BIND_ON_IP=0.0.0.0 is the working dev path.

## [2026-06-04] create | 6 raw + 6 concept pages — Banking RFP additional resources for online banking upgrade. Raw sources: raw/articles/banking-rfp-checklist-investglass.md, raw/articles/azure-landing-zone-financial-services.md, raw/articles/pci-dss-v4-0-1-banking-2026.md, raw/articles/wildfly-server-migration-tool.md, raw/articles/azure-policy-nist-sp-800-53-r5.md, raw/articles/banking-rfp-scoring-matrix.md. Concept pages: [[banking-rfp-framework]], [[azure-landing-zone-regulated-industries]], [[pci-dss-v4-0-1-requirements]], [[jboss-wildfly-migration-tool]], [[nist-sp-800-53-azure-policy-compliance]], [[rfp-scoring-matrix-methodology]]. Synthesized from InvestGlass banking RFP guide, Microsoft ALZ for Financial Services, PCI DSS v4.0.1 future-dated requirements (6.4.3 & 11.6.1), WildFly Server Migration Tool docs, Azure Policy NIST 800-53 Rev 5 initiative, and RFP scoring matrix methodology.
